Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [220] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kernel-WIS的新型估计器，用于基于离线数据的上下文赌博机离线策略评估。该估计器结合了加权重要性采样的有界性和普通重要性采样的线性性质，在行为策略误设定等复杂条件下，理论上渐近一致且经验上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线策略评估方法在复杂条件（如行为策略误设定）下表现不佳，标准重要性采样方差大，加权重要性采样有偏且非线性。需要一种兼具有界性和线性性质且渐近一致的估计器。

Method: 提出Kernel-WIS估计器，通过核函数技巧将加权重要性采样的有界性与普通重要性采样的线性性质相结合，实现渐近一致性。

Result: 理论证明Kernel-WIS渐近一致；实验上在行为策略误设定等复杂条件下，Kernel-WIS显著优于包括加权重要性采样在内的强基线方法。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS通过融合两种重要性采样的优势，提供了一种更鲁棒、更准确的离线策略评估方法，特别适用于行为策略存在误设定的实际场景。

Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

</details>


### [221] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了稀疏非线性动力学识别（SINDy）方法及其在工程问题中的应用教程，包括标准算法、噪声鲁棒变体、约束和参数化扩展，并通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个案例展示了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程问题中控制方程通常未知或仅部分已知，神经网络等代理模型需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理解释性，SINDy方法可通过稀疏回归从较小数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程，但其在工程实践中的应用文献较少。

Method: 采用SINDy方法及其扩展，包括噪声鲁棒的弱形式变体、基于集成的变体、约束和参数化公式，通过三部分组织教程：第一部分介绍标准SINDy算法并逐步扩展；后两部分呈现无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个详细案例研究。

Result: 通过两个工程案例（无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器）证明了SINDy方法实现简单、灵活性强，可作为高级工程应用中有价值的辨识工具。

Conclusion: SINDy方法简单易实现且足够灵活，能够作为工程应用中有价值的系统辨识工具，克服了神经网络等方法数据需求大和物理解释性差的问题。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [222] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在离线强化学习应用于治疗方案推荐时，使用协变量平衡诊断检测隐藏混杂/模型错误设定的可行性。结果表明，现有研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究。无论如何，现有离线RL研究无法被认定为统计稳健。结论提出了未来研究方法学改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在治疗方案推荐中的应用日益广泛，但隐藏混杂因素和模型错误设定可能导致有偏估计。现有研究缺乏对此类偏倚的系统性检测方法，亟需开发有效的诊断工具来评估研究结果的统计稳健性。

Method: 采用协变量平衡诊断方法，应用于离线RL治疗方案推荐研究，检测隐藏混杂和模型错误设定的存在性。通过分析现有研究中协变量平衡指标的表现，评估其是否能有效识别偏倚风险。

Result: 发现现有离线RL治疗方案推荐研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类偏倚。无论哪种情况，现有研究均无法被认定为统计稳健。

Conclusion: 指出了未来研究方向，旨在获得方法论上更稳健的离线RL治疗方案推荐应用，包括开发更有效的协变量平衡诊断工具和研究设计改进。

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>


### [223] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LINCS范畴框架，通过正切提升和余代数不动点来修复机器学习中的非组合性问题，将学习任务形式化为草图因式分解的障碍检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法通常依赖标量化损失函数或向量空间假设，缺乏处理非组合性（即图无法通过商草图因式分解）的统一形式化框架。本文旨在从范畴论角度提供严格的非组合性修复机制。

Method: (1) 将机器学习问题建模为草图$\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$，含交换条件、极限锥和余极限余锥；(2) 定义基础缺陷$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$作为因式分解的障碍；(3) 引入正切提升$TD$，定义LINCS为$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$；(4) 提出INC自函子迭代正切提升构造塔$D,TD,T^2D,\cdots$；(5) 利用Aczel-Mendler定理证明最终INC余代数的存在性。

Result: 建立了LINCS范畴框架，严格定义了非组合性为普适因式分解问题的失败而非算术误差；证明了在$T_{\mbox{INC}}$存在基于集合的类实现且创建最终载体的条件下，最终INC余代数存在；将机器学习问题转化为余代数不动点搜索问题。

Conclusion: LINCS为机器学习中的非组合性问题提供了严格的范畴论解决方案，通过正切提升和余代数框架将学习任务统一为因式分解障碍检测与不动点搜索，为深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习等场景提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [224] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)的新颖强化学习蒸馏方法，通过引入delta信号（教师模型与其基础模型在推理指令微调前的差异）替代直接模仿教师输出分布，在数学、科学和代码推理基准上显著优于传统on-policy蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy蒸馏方法虽然被广泛应用，但其基本设计仍未被充分探索。直接模仿教师模型的输出分布可能不是最优策略，需要一种更直接的信号来传递推理能力。

Method: 提出delta信号，定义为教师模型与其经过推理能力指令微调前的基础模型之间的差异。该信号捕捉了推理调优带来的变化，以此作为蒸馏奖励来指导强化学习训练。新方法称为On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理基准上的实验表明，OPD²一致性地优于传统on-policy蒸馏方法，使推理大语言模型在仅需短期间训练后即可达到强劲性能。

Conclusion: delta信号提供了一种更有效的教师知识传递方式，通过捕捉推理调优带来的变化而非直接模仿输出分布，显著提升了on-policy蒸馏的效果。该方法代码将开源。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [225] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合神经-物理框架，在部分状态可观测的情况下，将已知ODE分量保留为显式形式，用神经网络表示缺失分量，通过交替进行状态/参数估计（RTS平滑器+反向传播）来学习未知动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际动力系统中存在未知方程且仅部分状态可测，需要一种既能利用已知物理结构又能学习缺失动态的方法，同时保持模型的可解释性。

Method: 两阶段迭代方法：(1) 固定模型参数，利用RTS平滑器从可观测测量中推断潜在状态；(2) 固定平滑轨迹，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。交替进行直至收敛。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学的基准系统上，该方法能从部分观测数据中学习缺失的ODE分量，改善潜在状态重建和长期预测性能。

Conclusion: 混合神经-物理框架能够有效利用已知物理结构，同时通过神经网络灵活学习未知动态，保持可解释的机制结构，适用于部分观测下的动力系统建模。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [226] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BadWAM框架，揭示世界-动作模型（WAM）存在一种新型对抗攻击——世界-动作漂移攻击。通过两种攻击方式（动作攻击和想象保持攻击），BadWAM证明了WAM中动作生成与世界预测之间的对齐是脆弱的，能够被微小视觉扰动所破坏，导致任务失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM通过耦合动作生成与未来世界预测来提升鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性，但作者质疑这种耦合假设的可靠性，旨在揭示WAM特有的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出BadWAM统一框架，定义了两类攻击：（1）动作攻击 - 直接迫使模型产生导致任务失败的动作；（2）想象保持攻击 - 在保持未来预测接近原始结果的同时，诱导有害动作偏移。两种攻击覆盖从显式动作劫持到隐式漂移的完整攻击谱系。

Result: 攻击显著降低WAM在闭环执行下的任务成功率，动作攻击将成功率从96.5%降至43.1%；想象保持攻击进一步揭示了WAM特有的脆弱性。

Conclusion: WAM中动作生成与世界预测之间的对齐并非稳健，存在可被利用的安全漏洞，为WAM的安全部署提出了重要警示。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

</details>


### [227] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对块替换策略下多台独立同构机器的维护问题，提出数据驱动的多臂老虎机算法，在寿命分布未知时学习最优替换间隔 k*，实现了 O(K log T) 的遗憾界，并利用块替换的嵌套观测性质进一步改进为 O((K-k*) log T)，同时提出 Kaplan-Meier 更新算法实现非参数分布估计与策略一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际维护决策中机器寿命分布通常未知，传统块替换策略依赖于已知分布假设，需要从带删失的操作数据中自适应学习最优替换间隔以最小化长期平均成本。

Method: (1) 将问题建模为随机多臂老虎机，提出基于 Hoeffding 和 Bernstein 的 LCB 算法；(2) 利用块替换特有的嵌套观测性质，设计相关变体算法；(3) 提出 Kaplan-Meier 更新算法，从删失数据中非参数估计寿命分布；(4) 建立两个平均成本 MDP 模型（时间流逝与年龄向量）进行理论分析。

Result: (1) 基础算法实现 O(K log T) 遗憾，匹配 Lai-Robbins 下界；(2) 相关变体算法实现 O((K-k*) log T) 遗憾，且对 k<k* 仅需 O(1) 次直接拉取；(3) Kaplan-Meier 算法几乎必然策略一致，长期接近零增量遗憾；(4) MDP 分析证明块替换在其策略类中的最优性及单调阈值结构。

Conclusion: 本文为未知寿命分布下的块替换维护提供了理论最优的在线学习算法，数值实验验证了理论排序并揭示了块替换与年龄依赖替换之间的结构性成本差距。

Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

</details>


### [228] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可变草图（Mutable Sketches），利用KP树（稀疏分段树）存储用户偏好，通过一次性低秩投影和实时嵌入更新，解决两阶段推荐中的嵌入陈旧问题。在KuaiRec数据集上，仅用1.8%数据读取达到0.810 RMSE（ALS为0.822/100%数据），新用户首次评分后<1ms即可获得个性化推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段推荐系统中存在嵌入陈旧瓶颈：用户对新物品的评分后，其嵌入表示需等到下一个重训练周期才能更新，导致实时性差。

Method: 提出可变草图结构：用KP树（带求和聚合的稀疏分段树）存储用户偏好，仅需一次低秩投影拟合，评分到达时即时重算嵌入。

Result: KuaiRec上可变草图在1.8%数据读取下RMSE为0.810，优于ALS的0.822（100%数据）；每批更新速度提升8倍；新用户首评后<1ms获得推荐；KP树基于范数的采样策略在稀疏数据（<1%密度）上物品覆盖度提升40-130%。

Conclusion: 证明了每个新观测值单调收紧预测误差包络（定理1），这是FunkSVD和eALS所缺乏的保证。同时给出了不同密度场景下的采样策略建议：稀疏数据用KP树范数比例采样，稠密矩阵用均匀采样即可。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>


### [229] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体推文的新方法，用于分析比特币市场情绪。通过XGBoost模型分类情绪，F1-score约0.84，并使用SHAP增强模型可解释性。结果表明数据组合能提供有意义的预测信号，支持数据驱动的加密货币分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币作为去中心化数字资产和投资工具的日益普及，激发了对理解其市场行为的强烈兴趣。现有研究多关注价格预测，而本文聚焦于解释市场情绪。

Method: 融合区块链交易数据、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情绪分类，将情绪趋势与链上和金融指标归一化为数据集。使用多种机器学习模型进行交叉验证，采用XGBoost作为最佳分类模型，并运用SHAP量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献。

Result: XGBoost模型在情绪分类任务中表现最可靠，平均F1-score约0.84。SHAP分析成功量化了链上特征的重要性，提升了模型透明度。

Conclusion: 该方法中多种数据源的组合能产生有意义的预测信号和洞察，支持数据驱动的加密货币分析，并为未来引入深度学习改进提供了基础。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [ExaGEMM: Exploration Framework for CPU-Driven ML Inference via Associative In-Register Computing for Low-Bit GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14622)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ExaGEMM是一个面向CPU的低比特GEMM协同设计与探索框架，通过寄存器驻留LUT执行和轻量级SIMD ISA支持，在混合精度场景下实现13.29倍加速，并通过分析模型剪枝99.2%的候选空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特GEMM在高效ML推理中日益重要，但极低比特执行在传统CPU上效果不佳。实际部署涵盖1/2/4位权重及不同激活精度的碎片化场景，在固定SIMD和寄存器文件预算下，不同方案的可行性、复用机会和支持成本各异，使得轻量级CPU支持选择成为首要设计问题。

Method: 利用现有SIMD数据路径覆盖表生成和累加，仅新增寄存器内选择/馈送机制并显式建模成本。通过分析模型对寄存器可行性、计算成本、内存流量和硬件开销进行建模，在仿真前剪枝99.2%候选空间，识别非支配支持点并生成ISA规范、gem5补丁和GEMM内核用于验证。

Result: 在代表性ML模型和CPU目标上，相比纯软件基线实现13.29倍延迟提升，并表明工作负载感知的前沿选择对混合精度LLM工作负载尤为重要。

Conclusion: ExaGEMM证明了通过硬件-算法协同设计和系统化的候选空间探索，可以有效解决低比特GEMM在CPU上的部署难题，尤其适用于混合精度场景。

Abstract: Low-bit GEMM is increasingly central to efficient ML inference, yet very-low-bit execution remains a poor fit for conventional CPUs. Practical deployment spans fragmented regimes-from 1/2/4-bit weights to varying activation precision-whose feasibility, reuse opportunity, and support cost differ under fixed SIMD and register-file budgets, making lightweight CPU support selection a first-class design problem. We present ExaGEMM, a workload-aware codesign and exploration framework for CPU-native low-bit GEMM via register-resident LUT execution. The key insight is that existing SIMD datapaths already cover table generation and accumulation; the only new hardware is an in-register select/feed mechanism with explicitly modeled cost. ExaGEMM co-explores parameterized kernels and lightweight SIMD ISA support using analytical models of register feasibility, compute cost, memory traffic, and hardware overhead, pruning the candidate space by 99.2% before simulation. It then identifies non-dominated support points and generates ISA specs, gem5 patches, and GEMM kernels for validation. Across representative ML models and CPU targets, ExaGEMM improves latency by 13.29x over software-only baselines, while showing that workload-aware frontier selection is especially important for mixed-precision LLM workloads.

</details>


### [231] [Toward Energy-Efficient and Low-Power Arrhythmia Detection for Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14747)
*Floriaan Bulten,Yawar Rasheed,Arlene John,Vincenzo Stoico,Ghayoor Gillani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在深度学习心律失常分类模型中应用近似计算技术（数据精度降低和近似乘法器），在MIT-BIH心律失常数据库上训练验证，硬件实现结果显示在12kHz下功耗降低64.9%（3.07 μW），100MHz下能耗降低61.5%（0.8 μJ），同时保持93.7%的分类准确率和92.1%的灵敏度，有效延长了可穿戴设备电池寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可穿戴心律失常监测设备体积大、不舒适，且依赖临床医生手动评估心电图；深度学习算法虽有优异性能，但其计算复杂度和高功耗限制了在可穿戴设备上的部署。因此，需要在不显著降低分类性能的前提下降低深度学习架构的功耗和能耗。

Method: 采用近似计算技术，包括数据精度降低和近似乘法器，应用于先进的深度学习模型及其对应硬件架构。使用MIT-BIH心律失常数据库进行模型训练和验证，并综合评估了采用各种近似乘法器的硬件实现。

Result: 提出架构在12kHz下功耗3.07 μW（能耗2.17 μJ），相比基线架构功耗降低64.9%；在100MHz下功耗9.45 mW（能耗0.8 μJ），能耗降低61.5%。同时保持93.7%的分类准确率和92.1%的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 所提出的近似技术能够显著延长可穿戴设备的电池寿命，同时保持所需的心律失常分类性能，为深度学习在可穿戴健康监测设备中的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases are the leading cause of death worldwide, and conditions such as arrhythmia often require long-term monitoring for effective detection and diagnosis. However, current wearable monitoring devices are bulky, uncomfortable, and typically rely on clinicians to manually evaluate electrocardiograms (ECGs). While Deep Learning (DL) algorithms have shown superior performance in arrhythmia detection and classification, their computational complexity coupled with high power consumption limit deployment in wearable devices. To address this challenge, this paper investigates the use of approximation techniques to reduce the power and energy consumption of DL architectures while maintaining acceptable classification performance. Specifically, techniques such as data precision reduction and approximate multiplication are investigated in a state-of-the-art DL model and its corresponding hardware architecture. The model is trained and validated using the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database, and hardware implementations employing various approximate multipliers are synthesized and evaluated. Compared with the state-of-the-art 8.75 μW (and 2.08 μJ) reference architecture, our proposed architecture consumes 3.07 μW (and 2.17 μJ) at 12 kHz, showing 64.9% reduction in power consumption while providing an acceptable output quality, i.e., 93.7% classification accuracy and 92.1% sensitivity. At 100 MHz, our proposed architecture consumes 9.45 mW (and 0.8 μJ), showing 61.5% reduction in energy consumption as compared to the state-of-the-art architecture. These results demonstrate that our proposed approximations significantly extend wearable device battery life while preserving the required arrhythmia classification performance.

</details>


### [232] [Valinor: Architectural Support for Fast, Energy-Efficient and Programmable Physical Memory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14789)
*Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,Spiros Galanopoulos,Konstantinos Sgouras,Vlad-Petru Nitu,Ilias Papalamprou,Andreas Kosmas Kakolyris,Rahul Bera,Dimosthenis Masouros,Dimitrios Soudris,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Valinor 是一种硬件-操作系统协作的内存分配方案，通过可编程硬件分配引擎执行OS提供的分配库，在保持软件灵活性的同时实现接近固定硬件的性能，加速分配17倍，系统性能提升16%，能耗降低8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前系统中，每次缺页异常都会陷入内核并触发流水线冲刷、停顿以及大量分配步骤，耗费数万周期。对于无服务器函数和微服务等短生命期工作负载，缺页异常可占运行时高达54%和系统能耗40%。现有硬件分配方案要么牺牲有用的放置优化，要么依赖无法适应新策略或硬件条件变化的固定功能逻辑。

Method: Valinor 引入可编程硬件分配引擎，执行OS提供的紧凑型分配库，速度接近固定硬件。支持多样化策略，包括短生命期对象分配器、完整性机制和硬件遥测引导的放置优化。在BOOM RISC-V软核上运行Linux实现，并在全系统模拟器中进行评估。

Result: 在真实硬件上，分配加速17倍，端到端性能提升16%，能耗降低达8%。全系统模拟进一步验证了可编程分配引擎和六个分配库在保持可编程性的同时提供硬件级性能。

Conclusion: Valinor 证明了通过硬件-OS协作设计，可以在不牺牲软件灵活性的前提下获得硬件级别的内存分配性能，有效解决了短生命期工作负载中缺页异常带来的性能与能耗瓶颈。

Abstract: Physical memory allocation establishes virtual-to-physical mappings on demand. In current systems, each minor page fault traps into the kernel and triggers pipeline flushes, stalls, and a long sequence of allocation steps that can cost tens of thousands of cycles. These overheads are increasingly significant for short-lived workloads such as serverless functions and microservices, where minor faults can account for up to 54% of runtime and up to 40% of system energy. Prior hardware allocation proposals avoid traps and context switches, but either sacrifice useful placement optimizations or rely on fixed-function logic that cannot adapt to new policies or changing hardware conditions.
  We present Valinor, a hardware-OS cooperative memory allocation substrate that combines software flexibility with hardware-class performance. Valinor introduces a programmable hardware allocation engine that executes compact OS-supplied allocation libraries at close to fixed-hardware speed. It supports diverse policies, including short-lived object allocators, integrity mechanisms, and hardware-telemetry-guided placement. We implement Valinor on a BOOM RISC-V soft core running Linux and in a full-system simulator. On real hardware, Valinor accelerates allocation by 17x, improves end-to-end performance by 16%, and reduces energy consumption by up to 8%. Full-system simulation further evaluates the programmable allocation engine and six allocation libraries, showing that Valinor provides hardware-class performance without sacrificing programmability.

</details>


### [233] [CODA: Algorithm-Hardware Co-design for Edge Video Diffusion via NMP-Enabled Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14908)
*Yuanpeng Zhang,YuXuan Wu,Yitong Xiao,Chenhao Xue,Yi Ren,Cong Li,Yihan Yin,Dimin Niu,Guangyu Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CODA是一种算法-硬件协同设计架构，通过将计算与缓存操作解耦，在边缘设备上加速视频扩散模型的去噪过程，实现了最高1.80倍端到端加速和1.74倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存受限的边缘GPU上部署视频扩散模型时，跨时间步缓存(CTC)虽能减少冗余计算，但巨大的缓存占用超出设备VRAM，被迫使用主机内存；同时缓存与原生计算算子紧密交织且存在链式依赖，导致近内存卸载时产生频繁的PCIe通信和序列化瓶颈。

Method: 提出CODA架构，核心是计算-缓存算子解耦：将密集计算路径和内存受限的缓存路径分离到xPU和轻量级DIMM端近内存引擎；将碎片化缓存活动重组为硬件友好的合并段；利用无分类器引导(CFG)分支独立性实现xPU计算与缓存端执行的重叠。

Result: 相比最先进的缓存算法，CODA实现最高1.80倍端到端加速、1.74倍能效提升，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: CODA通过算法-硬件协同设计有效解决了边缘设备上视频扩散模型的缓存瓶颈，验证了计算-缓存算子解耦策略在内存受限场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Deploying Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) on edge devices is appealing for localized and privacy-preserving generation, but their iterative Transformer-based denoising remains too slow for practical local inference. Cross-Timestep Caching (CTC) has emerged as a promising direction for reducing redundant computation, reusing activations across adjacent denoising steps rather than modifying model weights, while largely preserving generation fidelity. However, on memory-constrained edge GPUs, CTC requires a massive cache footprint that quickly exceeds on-device VRAM and forces the cache into host memory. More fundamentally, cache operators remain tightly interleaved and chain-dependent with native compute operators, so naive near-memory offloading still incurs repeated PCIe exchanges for residual and fusion computations, turning cache reuse into a communication- and serialization-bound execution flow. We therefore propose CODA, an algorithm-hardware co-designed architecture centered on Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation. CODA separates dense compute paths and memory-bound cache paths across the xPU and a lightweight DIMM-side near-memory engine, reorganizes fragmented cache activity into hardware-friendly coalesced segments, and exploits Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) branch independence to overlap xPU compute with cache-side execution. Experiments show that CODA achieves up to 1.80x end-to-end speedup and 1.74x higher energy efficiency, while preserving competitive generation quality compared with a state-of-the-art caching algorithm.

</details>


### [234] [Differentiable Routability-Driven Package Floorplanning with Pin Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15005)
*Yiqi Huang,Zepeng Li,Zhen Zhuang,Kehao Chen,Genggeng Liu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向先进封装扇出路由的可微驱动布图规划与引脚分配算法，通过可微线长优化、交叉感知引脚分配和扇出定制拥塞模型，在全部测试集上实现100%可布通性，并比领先方法减少高达23%的线长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进封装中RDL互连密度不断增大，可布线性成为封装布图规划的关键；同时电源完整性要求保留扇入区给PDN，迫使信号网走扇出区，而现有均匀网格拥塞模型无法准确表征扇出拥塞，已有引脚分配方法也难以评估网交叉问题。

Method: 1）可微线长最小化方法，直接建模离散芯片朝向并反向传播线长梯度到芯片位置和朝向；2）交叉感知引脚分配，将网交叉代价融入多策略DPSO算法并利用GPU并行代价评估；3）可微可布线性最大化方法，构建针对扇出路由的定制拥塞模型并建立拥塞信息到芯片位置的反向传播路径。

Result: 在所有测试集上实现100%可布通性；在基线方法能成功布线的案例上，相比配备所提引脚分配流程的领先布图规划方法，线长减少高达约23%。

Conclusion: 所提出的可微驱动方法能有效解决先进封装中扇出路由的布图规划与引脚分配问题，在保证完全可布通的同时显著优化线长。

Abstract: As advanced packaging technology evolves, increasing interconnect density in redistribution layers (RDLs) makes routability critical to package floorplanning. Meanwhile, power integrity requirements often reserve fan-in regions for the power delivery network (PDN), forcing signal nets through fan-out regions and complicating routability estimation. Existing uniform grid-based congestion models cannot accurately characterize fan-out congestion, while previous pin assignment methods struggle to evaluate net crossings. We propose a differentiable routability-driven floorplanning and pin assignment algorithm for advanced packaging with fan-out routing. First, a differentiable wirelength minimization method directly models discrete chip orientations and back-propagates wirelength gradients to chip locations and orientations. It reduces wirelength under fixed pin selection while avoiding the bias of continuous-angle modeling. Second, a crossing-aware pin assignment method incorporates net-crossing cost into a multi-strategy DPSO algorithm and uses GPU-parallel cost evaluation to reduce wirelength efficiently. Finally, a differentiable routability maximization method constructs a congestion model tailored to fan-out routing and establishes a back-propagation path from congestion information to chip locations, thereby guiding routability optimization. Experimental results show that our method achieves 100% routability on all benchmarks. For cases successfully routed by the baselines, it reduces wirelength by up to approximately 23% compared with a leading floorplanning method equipped with our pin assignment flow.

</details>


### [235] [Pattern-Guided Design Space Exploration for FPGA Accelerator Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15068)
*Jialiang Zhang,Weiman Yan,Yuelin Zou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PATTERNDSE 是一个轻量级模式引导的设计空间探索框架，通过识别FPGA内核中的重复计算模式（如逐元素映射、归约、矩阵运算和模板更新）来压缩调度空间，在Vitis HLS合成前利用LLVM执行验证和模式感知评估器排序候选方案。实验表明，相比穷举基线方法，该方法将需要HLS评估的候选方案从140个减少到29个（整体搜索缩减4.83倍，单个内核最高12倍），同时恢复了相同的最佳有效Vitis HLS延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层次综合（HLS）将FPGA加速器设计抽象到C/C++级别，但高质量结果仍依赖于流水线、展开、分块、重排序和缓冲等调度决策，这些决策形成了组合暴增的设计空间。许多数值内核表现出重复的计算模式，这些模式暗示了不同的优化策略，因此需要利用模式信息来引导设计空间探索，减少不必要的搜索开销。

Method: 提出PATTERNDSE框架：1) 将重复计算模式（逐元素映射、归约、矩阵-向量操作、矩阵-矩阵操作、模板更新）映射到紧凑的调度空间；2) 应用候选调度方案；3) 通过LLVM执行验证功能正确性；4) 检查HLS C代码生成；5) 使用简单的模式感知评估器在Vitis HLS合成前对候选方案进行排序。

Result: 在vecadd、axpy、dot、matvec、gemm和jacobi2d六个代表性内核上，相比穷举lite基线，模式引导的DSE将需要HLS评估的候选方案从140个减少到29个，整体搜索缩减4.83倍，单个内核最高达到12倍缩减，同时恢复了相同的延迟。

Conclusion: 计算模式信息可以有效剪枝非生产性的调度组合，同时保留高质量的HLS输出结果，验证了模式引导设计空间探索的有效性。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) raises the abstraction level of FPGA accelerator design from hardware description languages to C/C++, but high-quality results still depend on schedule decisions such as pipelining, unrolling, tiling, reordering, and buffering. These decisions create a combinatorial design space, while many numerical kernels exhibit recurring computation patterns that suggest different optimization strategies. This paper presents PATTERNDSE, a lightweight pattern-guided design space exploration (DSE) framework for FPGA kernels written in Allo, a scheduling-oriented HLS programming system. PATTERNDSE maps recurring computation patterns, including elementwise maps, reductions, matrix-vector operations, matrix-matrix operations, and stencil-like updates, to compact schedule spaces. It then applies candidate schedules, validates functional correctness through LLVM execution, checks HLS C code generation, and uses a simple pattern-aware estimator to rank candidates before Vitis HLS synthesis. We evaluate PATTERNDSE on six representative kernels: vecadd, axpy, dot, matvec, gemm, and jacobi2d. Compared with an exhaustive-lite baseline, pattern-guided DSE reduces the number of HLS-evaluated candidates from 140 to 29, achieving a 4.83x overall search reduction and up to 12.0x reduction for individual kernels. Across all evaluated kernels, PATTERNDSE recovers the same best valid Vitis HLS latency as the exhaustive-lite baseline, demonstrating that computation-pattern information can prune unproductive schedule combinations while preserving high-quality HLS outcomes.

</details>


### [236] [NIFA: Nonlinear IMC enhanced FPGA for efficient ML inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15123)
*Jiajun Hu,Ruthwik Reddy Sunketa,Lei Zhao,Archit Gajjar,Luca Buonanno,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对传统ReRAM存内计算(IMC)在FPGA上仅支持静态权值、依赖高功耗ADC、难以高效处理Transformer非线性与动态矩阵乘法(DIMM)等问题，提出一种无ADC的IMC块架构，采用模拟内容可寻址存储器(ACAM)替代ADC，支持块内非线性运算和DIMM映射，经FPGA感知的设计空间探索优化，在CNN和Transformer基准测试中分别实现最高40倍和1.9倍能效提升以及4.1倍和2.5倍面积效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IMC设计仅支持静态权值VMM，非线性运算和动态矩阵乘法需依赖FPGA逻辑实现，导致Transformer等依赖大量非线性和DIMM操作的模型收益有限；同时IMC块内ADC功耗和面积占比超70%，严重限制系统效率和可扩展性。

Method: 提出无ADC的IMC块，用ACAM替代ADC在块内原生执行非线性运算；进行FPGA感知的设计空间探索，确定最优交叉阵列维度；开发基于ACAM的高效映射方法实现DIMM运算，将IMC适用范围扩展到注意力计算。

Result: 在CNN基准上实现最高40倍能效提升和4.1倍面积效率提升；在Transformer基准上实现最高1.9倍能效提升和2.5倍面积效率提升；长序列输入下仍保持稳健增益。

Conclusion: 所提架构显著提升了FPGA上DL推理效率，尤其是对Transformer类工作负载具有持续优势，推进了领域专用FPGA设计。

Abstract: Recent FPGAs have improved deep learning (DL) inference efficiency through dedicated tensor blocks and in-BRAM computation. ReRAM-based analog in-memory computing (IMC) pushes efficiency further, offering an order-of-magnitude improvement in compute density and energy efficiency over conventional digital logic by performing vector-matrix multiplication (VMM) directly within the ReRAM crossbar; prior work has integrated such IMC blocks into FPGAs for DL inference. However, conventional IMC designs support only static-weight VMM, leaving nonlinear operations and dynamic matrix-matrix multiplication (DIMM) to the FPGA fabric. As a result, the benefits of IMC are largely confined to static-weight models, whereas Transformer-based models, which rely on frequent nonlinear and DIMM operations, gain only limited improvement. Moreover, the ADCs within each IMC block consume more than 70% of its area and power, further limiting system efficiency and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose a novel FPGA architecture that integrates an ADC-free IMC block, replacing the conventional ADC with analog content-addressable memories (ACAMs) that natively perform nonlinear operations inside the block. To fully exploit this block, we conduct an FPGA-aware design-space exploration that determines optimal crossbar dimensions while balancing FPGA area, flexibility, and DL performance, and we develop an efficient mapping that leverages ACAMs to carry out DIMM operations, extending the applicability of IMC to attention computation. On CNN and Transformer-based benchmarks, the proposed architecture achieves up to 40x and 1.9x higher energy efficiency and 4.1x and 2.5x higher area efficiency, respectively. Overall, it significantly improves FPGA DL inference efficiency and sustains robust gains on Transformer-based workloads across long input sequences, advancing domain-specialized FPGA design.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Campaign Diagrams: Visualizing the March Through the Phases of a Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15225)
*Toluwanimi O. Odemuyiwa,John D. Owens,Michael Pellauer,Joel S. Emer*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 提出"campaign diagrams"可视化技术，用于现代工作负载中资源利用和瓶颈的阶段级分析，通过单一图表展示计算吞吐量、内存带宽利用率、流量和延迟，弥补了屋顶线模型（丢失时间信息）和性能分析工具（掩盖瓶颈）之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具存在权衡：屋顶线模型将工作负载聚合成单一数据点，丢失了所有时间信息；而性能分析器和轨迹虽然暴露细粒度事件，但掩盖了性能瓶颈所在。需要一种能够同时保留时间信息并清晰展示瓶颈的可视化方法。

Method: 提出campaign diagrams可视化技术，在单一图表中同时呈现计算吞吐量、内存带宽利用率、计算和内存流量以及延迟。该图表可从分析模型、仿真或性能分析数据生成，能够同时捕捉理想边界和内核的实际性能。

Result: 通过两个案例研究验证：1) 低秩GEMM中，揭示了降低运算强度反而提升端到端性能的反直觉结果；2) Mamba中，暴露了跨阶段的融合和流水线优化机会。在这两种情况下，该可视化技术揭示了难以通过屋顶线模型或性能分析工具单独识别的优化机会。

Conclusion: Campaign diagrams是一种有效的阶段级性能分析可视化技术，能够揭示其他工具难以发现的优化机会，弥补了现有性能分析工具在时间信息和瓶颈识别之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We present campaign diagrams, a visualization technique for phase-level analysis of resource utilization and bottlenecks in modern workloads. Existing tools have a trade-off: rooflines aggregate a workload into a single point and lose all notion of time, while profilers and traces expose fine-grained events but obscure what bounds performance. Instead, a campaign diagram depicts compute throughput and memory bandwidth utilization, compute and memory traffic volume, and latency in a single figure. Since they can be generated from analytical models, simulations, or profiling data, campaign diagrams capture both ideal bounds and a kernel's achieved performance. We demonstrate them on two case studies: a low-rank GEMM, where they reveal the counterintuitive result that reducing operational intensity can improve end-to-end performance, and Mamba, where they expose fusion and pipelining opportunities across phases. In both cases, our visualization technique reveals optimization opportunities that are difficult to identify with rooflines or profilers alone.

</details>
